# ---- 核心圈入口国策：7 名候选人各一个，完成即办理入席 ----
# 由 build/gen-hof-ic-s3.ps1 生成，勿手改。
# 前置：已完成「最高苏维埃」(SOV_the_supreme_soviet)
# available：任命冷却已过，且本人不在席（满席不再挡路，改由替换事件处理）
# 效果：调用 hof_ic_appoint_<key>，内部决定"直接坐"或"走替换事件"
# ⚠️ available 末尾的 NOT 块 = 席位互斥，由 docs/10 §2.4.2 的三条规则展开而来。
#    不要手改；要改改生成器顶部的 $camps / $crossRules / $pairRules。

focus = {
	id = hof_ic_commissar
# 图标复用原版「最高苏维埃」那张（用户 2026-09-16 定，不另出图）
	icon = GFX_focus_SOV_the_supreme_soviet
	prerequisite = { focus = SOV_the_supreme_soviet }
	relative_position_id = SOV_the_supreme_soviet
	x = 0
	y = 2
	cost = 1
	available = {
		has_government = communism
	}
	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_commissar_inner_circle_tt
		add_political_power = 50
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism
			popularity = 0.05
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_skip_line_tt
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader_with_trait = strong_party_discipline }
			SOV_supreme_soviet = {
				swap_country_leader_traits = {
					remove = strong_party_discipline
					add = hof_ic_commissar_party_discipline
				}
			}
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			# 核心圈容器（整个核心圈唯一一个可见民族精神）
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = hof_ic_supreme_soviet_dm }
		}
	}
}

focus = {
	id = hof_ic_trotsky_build_the_red_army
	icon = GFX_hof_ic_trotsky_build_the_red_army
	prerequisite = {
		focus = hof_ic_seat_lev_trotsky
	}
	relative_position_id = hof_ic_seat_lev_trotsky
	x = -8
	y = 1
	cost = 2
	available = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_lev_trotsky_progress has_shine_effect_on_focus = hof_ic_trotsky_build_the_red_army }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_in_progress_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_lev_trotsky
					FLAG_DAYS = [?hof_ic_lev_trotsky_progress:days]
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_advisor_only_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_lev_trotsky
					FOCUS = hof_ic_seat_lev_trotsky
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
	}
	completion_reward = {
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_trotsky_build_the_red_army }
		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_trotsky_extra_effects_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_lev_trotsky_army_org = 0.05
			tooltip = army_org_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_lev_trotsky_training_time = -0.05
			tooltip = training_time_factor_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = { hof_ic_role_refresh_lev_trotsky = yes }
	}
}

			}
		}
	}

	icon = GFX_hof_ic_zinovyev_monolithic_international
	mutually_exclusive = { focus = hof_ic_zinovyev_mass_international }
	prerequisite = {
		focus = hof_ic_zinovyev_bolshevization
		focus = hof_ic_zinovyev_speed_and_route
		focus = hof_ic_zinovyev_trade_unions
	}
	relative_position_id = hof_ic_seat_grigory_zinovyev
	x = -1
	y = 6
	cost = 2
	available = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { SOV_grigory_zinovyev = { has_character_flag = hof_ic_zinovyev_ending_monolithic_flag } } }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_event_choice_only_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_grigory_zinovyev
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_grigory_zinovyev_progress has_shine_effect_on_focus = hof_ic_zinovyev_monolithic_international }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_in_progress_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_grigory_zinovyev
					FLAG_DAYS = [?hof_ic_grigory_zinovyev_progress:days]
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_advisor_only_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_grigory_zinovyev
					FOCUS = hof_ic_seat_grigory_zinovyev
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
	}
	completion_reward = {
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_zinovyev_monolithic_international }
		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_zinovyev_extra_effects_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_grigory_zinovyev_political_power = 0.10
			tooltip = political_power_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_grigory_zinovyev_drift_defence = 0.15
			tooltip = drift_defence_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_grigory_zinovyev_stability = 0.05
			tooltip = stability_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_grigory_zinovyev_communism_drift = 0.02
			tooltip = communism_drift_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = { hof_ic_role_refresh_grigory_zinovyev = yes }
	}
}

	icon = GFX_hof_ic_litvinov_the_litvinov_protocol
	prerequisite = {
		focus = hof_ic_litvinov_the_prisoner_exchange_agreement
	}
	relative_position_id = hof_ic_seat_maksim_litvinov
	x = 0
	y = 3
	cost = 2
	available = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_progress has_shine_effect_on_focus = hof_ic_litvinov_the_litvinov_protocol }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_in_progress_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_maksim_litvinov
					FLAG_DAYS = [?hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_progress:days]
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_advisor_only_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_maksim_litvinov
					FOCUS = hof_ic_seat_maksim_litvinov
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
	}
	completion_reward = {
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_litvinov_the_litvinov_protocol }
		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_litvinov_extra_effects_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_political_power = 0.05
			tooltip = political_power_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_opinion_gain_monthly = 0.05
			tooltip = hof_ic_tt_opinion_gain_monthly_factor
		}
		hidden_effect = { hof_ic_role_refresh_maksim_litvinov = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		# 升官衔：重算等级（照德国 GER_add_*_effects 的反推法）
		custom_effect_tooltip = {
			localization_key = hof_ic_rank_up_tt
			CHARACTER = SOV_maksim_litvinov
		}
		effect_tooltip = {   # 照德国：引擎自己写「以 hof_ic_idea_maksim_litvinov_3 取代 hof_ic_idea_maksim_litvinov_2」+「效果变化:」
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = hof_ic_idea_maksim_litvinov_2
				add_idea = hof_ic_idea_maksim_litvinov_3
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { hof_ic_idea_maksim_litvinov_sync_rank = yes }
	}
}

focus = {
	id = hof_ic_litvinov_the_franco_soviet_treaty
	icon = GFX_hof_ic_litvinov_the_franco_soviet_treaty
	prerequisite = {
		focus = hof_ic_litvinov_the_definition_of_aggression
	}
	relative_position_id = hof_ic_seat_maksim_litvinov
	x = 0
	y = 5
	cost = 2
	available = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_progress has_shine_effect_on_focus = hof_ic_litvinov_the_franco_soviet_treaty }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_in_progress_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_maksim_litvinov
					FLAG_DAYS = [?hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_progress:days]
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_advisor_only_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_maksim_litvinov
					FOCUS = hof_ic_seat_maksim_litvinov
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
	}
	completion_reward = {
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_litvinov_the_franco_soviet_treaty }
		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_litvinov_extra_effects_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_opinion_gain_monthly = 0.05
			tooltip = hof_ic_tt_opinion_gain_monthly_factor
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_maksim_litvinov_improve_relations_maintain_cost = -0.05
			tooltip = hof_ic_tt_improve_relations_maintain_cost_factor
		}
		hidden_effect = { hof_ic_role_refresh_maksim_litvinov = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = { is_subject = no  NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
			}
			diplomatic_relation = { country = FRA  relation = non_aggression_pact  active = yes }
			give_guarantee = FRA
			add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = hof_ic_franco_soviet_treaty  target = FRA }
			FRA = {
				diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT  relation = non_aggression_pact  active = yes }
				give_guarantee = ROOT
				add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = hof_ic_franco_soviet_treaty  target = ROOT }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { CZE = { exists = yes } }
			CZE = { add_opinion_modifier = { modifier = hof_ic_soviet_czech_treaty  target = ROOT } }
		}
	}
}

	icon = GFX_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan
	prerequisite = {
		focus = hof_ic_seat_nikolay_voznesensky
	}
	relative_position_id = hof_ic_seat_nikolay_voznesensky
	x = 0
	y = 1
	cost = 2
	available = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = hof_ic_nikolay_voznesensky_progress has_shine_effect_on_focus = hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan }
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_in_progress_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_nikolay_voznesensky
					FLAG_DAYS = [?hof_ic_nikolay_voznesensky_progress:days]
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = {
					localization_key = hof_ic_focus_advisor_only_tt
					CHARACTER = SOV_nikolay_voznesensky
					FOCUS = hof_ic_seat_nikolay_voznesensky
				}
				always = no
			}
		}
	}
	completion_reward = {
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = hof_ic_done_hof_ic_voznesensky_the_fight_for_the_plan }
		custom_effect_tooltip = hof_ic_voznesensky_extra_effects_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_nikolay_voznesensky_production_factory_max_efficiency = 0.05
			tooltip = production_factory_max_efficiency_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_nikolay_voznesensky_political_power = 0.05
			tooltip = political_power_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_nikolay_voznesensky_stability = 0.05
			tooltip = stability_factor_tt
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			hof_ic_nikolay_voznesensky_industrial_capacity = 0.05
			tooltip = industrial_capacity_factory_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = { hof_ic_role_refresh_nikolay_voznesensky = yes }
	}
}

# 中间国策「人民委员」—— 核心圈的枢纽（照德国 GER_fuhrerprinzip）
#   所有入口国策都挂在它下面（prerequisite + relative_position_id）,
#   所以整块核心圈的位置由这一个点决定：要整体下移/左右挪，只改这里。
#   ⚠️ 容器 DM 也在这里挂载 —— 完成这个国策才算真正开启核心圈机制。
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

focus = {
	id = hof_ic_new_soviet_chairman
